MODULE wcCycle — The cycle, and who is paying for it
THE QUESTION THIS PANEL ANSWERS: How long is the business's own money out, who is funding each part of that, and which days can be removed on evidence the source already holds?

FACTS (refer to these by id; never write a figure of your own)
  wcCycle.cycleDays | Working capital cycle | 342 days | lower is better | Everything tied up, over revenue per day. Every component divides by the same rate, so the parts add up exactly.
  wcCycle.deployed | Working capital deployed | ₹18 Cr | lower is better
  wcCycle.coveredDays | Funded by others | 108.2 days | BENCHMARK | The client's advance and vendor credit. The cycle funds itself at this length — reaching it is the objective.
  wcCycle.reserveDays | Funded from the business's own reserves | 233.8 days | lower is better | The only part that carries a financing cost.
  wcCycle.ownFunds | Own money deployed | ₹12.3 Cr | lower is better
  wcCycle.annualCarry | Annual carry on reserves | ₹1.14 Cr | lower is better
  wcCycle.removableDays | Removable on evidence | 65.9 days | Days measured against this contract's own agreed terms or a leg the business owns end to end.
  wcCycle.afterActions | Cycle after every evidenced action | 276.1 days | BENCHMARK, lower is better
  wcCycle.structuralGap | Structural gap left after all actions | 167.9 days | Needs a larger advance, a shorter billing cycle or different terms — not better execution.
  wcCycle.revenuePerDay | One day of cycle, in cash | ₹5.26 L
  wcCycle.zeroRemovable | Every evidenced day removed | 0 days | BENCHMARK | The floor for any of the action figures below — the leg back inside the term this contract already agreed.
  wcCycle.action.collection | Collect inside the agreed payment window | 23.2 days | lower is better | Client — escalate the overdue, by size. Measured against the 30-day payment term in this contract. Confidence high.
  wcCycle.action.certification | Bring certification back to the window the contract already agreed | 22.8 days | lower is better | Client — with the contract behind it. Measured against the 15-day certification window in this contract. Confidence high.
  wcCycle.action.material | Stop material arriving ahead of the programme | 19.9 days | lower is better | Procurement and Stores — ours, needs nobody's permission. Measured against the days material sits before the work consumes it. Confidence high.

== PACK wcCycle
PROJECT GI-MMR-01 — Mumbai Metro Line 9 — Viaduct & Stations Package 04
AS AT 2027-03-20 (the last event the source has, never today)
FINANCING RATE 9.25% a year

MODULE wcCycle — The cycle, and who is paying for it
THE QUESTION THIS PANEL ANSWERS: How long is the business's own money out, who is funding each part of that, and which days can be removed on evidence the source already holds?

FACTS (refer to these by id; never write a figure of your own)
  wcCycle.cycleDays | Working capital cycle | 558.1 days | lower is better | Everything tied up, over revenue per day. Every component divides by the same rate, so the parts add up exactly.
  wcCycle.deployed | Working capital deployed | ₹72.6 Cr | lower is better
  wcCycle.coveredDays | Funded by others | 178.8 days | BENCHMARK | The client's advance and vendor credit. The cycle funds itself at this length — reaching it is the objective.
  wcCycle.reserveDays | Funded from the business's own reserves | 379.3 days | lower is better | The only part that carries a financing cost.
  wcCycle.ownFunds | Own money deployed | ₹49.3 Cr | lower is better
  wcCycle.annualCarry | Annual carry on reserves | ₹4.56 Cr | lower is better
  wcCycle.removableDays | Removable on evidence | 213.2 days | Days measured against this contract's own agreed terms or a leg the business owns end to end.
  wcCycle.afterActions | Cycle after every evidenced action | 344.8 days | BENCHMARK, lower is better
  wcCycle.structuralGap | Structural gap left after all actions | 166.1 days | Needs a larger advance, a shorter billing cycle or different terms — not better execution.
  wcCycle.revenuePerDay | One day of cycle, in cash | ₹13 L
  wcCycle.zeroRemovable | Every evidenced day removed | 0 days | BENCHMARK | The floor for any of the action figures below — the leg back inside the term this contract already agreed.
  wcCycle.action.certification | Bring certification back to the window the contract already agreed | 108.7 days | lower is better | Client — with the contract behind it. Measured against the 21-day certification window in this contract. Confidence high.
  wcCycle.action.collection | Collect inside the agreed payment window | 66.6 days | lower is better | Client — escalate the overdue, by size. Measured against the 60-day payment term in this contract. Confidence high.
  wcCycle.action.material | Stop material arriving ahead of the programme | 37.9 days | lower is better | Procurement and Stores — ours, needs nobody's permission. Measured against the days material sits before the work consumes it. Confidence medium.

WHAT THE CONTRACT AGREED (the only legitimate target source)

  Mobilisation advance: 10%

== PACK wcCycle
PROJECT GI-BSP-02 — Bhilai Steel Plant — Blast Furnace 8 Modernisation
AS AT 2027-03-20 (the last event the source has, never today)
FINANCING RATE 9.25% a year

MODULE wcCycle — The cycle, and who is paying for it
THE QUESTION THIS PANEL ANSWERS: How long is the business's own money out, who is funding each part of that, and which days can be removed on evidence the source already holds?

FACTS (refer to these by id; never write a figure of your own)
  wcCycle.cycleDays | Working capital cycle | 842.4 days | lower is better | Everything tied up, over revenue per day. Every component divides by the same rate, so the parts add up exactly.
  wcCycle.deployed | Working capital deployed | ₹87.3 Cr | lower is better
  wcCycle.coveredDays | Funded by others | 193.1 days | BENCHMARK | The client's advance and vendor credit. The cycle funds itself at this length — reaching it is the objective.
  wcCycle.reserveDays | Funded from the business's own reserves | 649.3 days | lower is better | The only part that carries a financing cost.
  wcCycle.ownFunds | Own money deployed | ₹67.3 Cr | lower is better
  wcCycle.annualCarry | Annual carry on reserves | ₹6.22 Cr | lower is better
  wcCycle.removableDays | Removable on evidence | 315.4 days | Days measured against this contract's own agreed terms or a leg the business owns end to end.
  wcCycle.afterActions | Cycle after every evidenced action | 527 days | BENCHMARK, lower is better
  wcCycle.structuralGap | Structural gap left after all actions | 333.9 days | Needs a larger advance, a shorter billing cycle or different terms — not better execution.
  wcCycle.revenuePerDay | One day of cycle, in cash | ₹10.4 L
  wcCycle.zeroRemovable | Every evidenced day removed | 0 days | BENCHMARK | The floor for any of the action figures below — the leg back inside the term this contract already agreed.
  wcCycle.action.certification | Bring certification back to the window the contract already agreed | 181.2 days | lower is better | Client — with the contract behind it. Measured against the 30-day certification window in this contract. Confidence high.
  wcCycle.action.collection | Collect inside the agreed payment window | 68.2 days | lower is better | Client — escalate the overdue, by size. Measured against the 90-day payment term in this contract. Confidence high.
  wcCycle.action.material | Stop material arriving ahead of the programme | 65.9 days | lower is better | Procurement and Stores — ours, needs nobody's permission. Measured against the days material sits before the work consumes it. Confidence medium.

FACTS (refer to these by id; never write a figure of your own)
  wc.workingCapital | Working capital | ₹101 Cr | lower is better
  wc.ccc | Cash cycle | 150.5 days | lower is better
  wc.dso | Days the client takes to pay | 158.2 days | lower is better
  wc.dio | Days material sits before use | 65.9 days | lower is better
  wc.dpo | Days we take to pay vendors | 73.6 days | neither direction is automatically good | Never read as higher-is-better. Long terms negotiated is good business; the same number from paying late is a risk.
  wc.contractAssetDays | Work built but not certified | 211.2 days | lower is better
  wc.surplus | Surplus till now | -₹19.3 Cr | higher is better

  wc.intensity | Share of the contract the business funds | 36.1% | lower is better

  wc.overdue | Invoiced, past its own due date, unpaid | ₹15.8 Cr | lower is better
  wc.zeroOverdue | Nothing past its due date | ₹0 | BENCHMARK | Every invoice collected inside the term the client agreed.
  wc.certificationTerm | Days the client agreed to certify within | 30 days | BENCHMARK | Signed in this contract. Not a benchmark from anywhere else.

== PACK wcCycle
PROJECT GI-PIT-03 — Hinjewadi IT Park — Tower C Core & Shell
AS AT 2027-03-20 (the last event the source has, never today)
FINANCING RATE 9.25% a year

MODULE wcCycle — The cycle, and who is paying for it
THE QUESTION THIS PANEL ANSWERS: How long is the business's own money out, who is funding each part of that, and which days can be removed on evidence the source already holds?

FACTS (refer to these by id; never write a figure of your own)
  wcCycle.cycleDays | Working capital cycle | 204.2 days | lower is better | Everything tied up, over revenue per day. Every component divides by the same rate, so the parts add up exactly.
  wcCycle.deployed | Working capital deployed | ₹24.3 Cr | lower is better
  wcCycle.coveredDays | Funded by others | 54.1 days | BENCHMARK | The client's advance and vendor credit. The cycle funds itself at this length — reaching it is the objective.
  wcCycle.reserveDays | Funded from the business's own reserves | 150.1 days | lower is better | The only part that carries a financing cost.
  wcCycle.ownFunds | Own money deployed | ₹17.8 Cr | lower is better
  wcCycle.annualCarry | Annual carry on reserves | ₹1.65 Cr | lower is better
  wcCycle.removableDays | Removable on evidence | 90.5 days | Days measured against this contract's own agreed terms or a leg the business owns end to end.
  wcCycle.afterActions | Cycle after every evidenced action | 113.7 days | BENCHMARK, lower is better
  wcCycle.structuralGap | Structural gap left after all actions | 59.6 days | Needs a larger advance, a shorter billing cycle or different terms — not better execution.
  wcCycle.revenuePerDay | One day of cycle, in cash | ₹11.9 L
  wcCycle.zeroRemovable | Every evidenced day removed | 0 days | BENCHMARK | The floor for any of the action figures below — the leg back inside the term this contract already agreed.
  wcCycle.action.certification | Bring certification back to the window the contract already agreed | 40.1 days | lower is better | Client — with the contract behind it. Measured against the 14-day certification window in this contract. Confidence high.
  wcCycle.action.collection | Collect inside the agreed payment window | 25.5 days | lower is better | Client — escalate the overdue, by size. Measured against the 30-day payment term in this contract. Confidence high.
  wcCycle.action.material | Stop material arriving ahead of the programme | 24.9 days | lower is better | Procurement and Stores — ours, needs nobody's permission. Measured against the days material sits before the work consumes it. Confidence medium.

WHAT THE CONTRACT AGREED (the only legitimate target source)

  Mobilisation advance: 5%
